Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\n// \n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n//        const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\\n// \\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\\n\\n#include <algorithm>\\n#include <chrono>\\n#include <cmath>\\n#include <cstdio>\\n#include <cstdlib>\\n#include <cstring>\\n#include <limits>\\n#include <vector>\\n\\n#include \\\"../common/results_output.hpp\\\"\\n\\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\\n\\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\\nstruct Point {\\n    double x, y;\\n};\\n\\n// Structure to represent a cluster\\nstruct Cluster {\\n    std::vector<int> members;\\n    int mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\\n    \\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\\n    int count = 0;\\n    \\n    while (count < N) {\\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\\n        \\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\\n            group_cnt = N - count;\\n        }\\n        \\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\\n            const double r = frand() * R;\\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\\n            \\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\\n                continue;\\n            }\\n            \\n            points[count] = {x, y};\\n            count++;\\n            group_cnt--;\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\\n}\\n\\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\\n                     const                 const int point_count) {\\n    int closest_point = -1;\\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\\n    \\n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\\n       \\n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\\n            closest_point = candidate;\\n        }\\n    }\\n    \\n    return closest_point;\\n}\\n\\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\\n// Returns the members.push_back(seed_point);\\n    \\n    // Iteratively add closest points\\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \\n                                             threshold, point_count);\\n        \\n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\\n        \\n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\\n        members.push_back(closest);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Copy members if requested\\n    if (cluster_members) {\\n        *cluster_members = members;\\n    }\\n    \\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\\n}\\n\\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                                  const double threshold) {\\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\\n    \\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Main clustering loop\\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\\n        int best_seed = cluster;\\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\\n            \\n            // Mark all members   \\n    // Check each cluster\\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\\n        \\n        // Check         }\\n        }\\n        \\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\\n            printf(\\\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\\\n\\\", \\n                   c,   }\\n    \\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < printf(\\\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\\\n\\\");\\n    printf(\\\"  -h           Show this help message\\\\n\\\");\\n}\\n\\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\\n    int     if (strcmp(argv[i], \\\"-n\\\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \\\"-t\\\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\\n            threshold QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\\n// \\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\\n\\n#include <algorithm>\\n#include <chrono>\\n#include <cmath>\\n#include <cstdio>\\n#include <cstdlib>\\n#include <cstring>\\n#include <limits>\\n#include <vector>\\n\\n#include \\\"../common/results_output.hpp\\\"\\n\\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\\n\\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\\nstruct Point {\\n    double x, y;\\n};\\n\\n// Structure to represent a cluster\\nstruct Cluster {\\n    std::vector<int> members;\\n    int seed_point;\\n};\\n\\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\\n    \\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\\n    int count = 0;\\n    \\n    while (count < N) {\\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\\n        \\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\\n            group_cnt = N - count;\\n        }\\n        \\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\\n            const double r = frand() * R;\\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\\n            \\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\\n                continue;\\n            }\\n            \\n            points[count] = {x, y};\\n            count++;\\n            group_cnt--;\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\\n}\\n\\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                     const double threshold,\\n                     const int point_count) {\\n    int closest_point = -1;\\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\\n    \\n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\\n        \\n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\\n        }\\n        \\n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\\n            closest_point = candidate;\\n        }\\n    }\\n    \\n    return closest_point;\\n}\\n\\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                              const double threshold,\\n                              const int point_count,\\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\\n    std::vector<int> members;\\n    \\n    // Add seed point\\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\\n    \\n    // Iteratively add closest points\\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \\n                                             threshold, point_count);\\n        \\n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\\n        \\n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\\n        members.push_back(closest);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Copy members if requested\\n    if (cluster_members) {\\n        *cluster_members = members;\\n    }\\n    \\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\\n}\\n\\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                                  const double threshold) {\\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\\n    \\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Main clustering loop\\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\\n        int best_seed = -1;\\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\\n        \\n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\\n            \\n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \\n                                                       threshold, N, \\n                                                       &candidate_members);\\n            \\n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\\n                best_seed = seed;\\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\\n            }\\n        }\\n        \\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\\n            Cluster cluster;\\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\\n            \\n            // Mark all members as clustered\\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\\n            }\\n            \\n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\\n                unclustered_indices.end()\\n            );\\n        } else {\\n            // No more clusters can be formed\\n            break;\\n        }\\n    }\\n    \\n    return clusters;\\n}\\n\\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                     const double threshold) {\\n    bool valid = true;\\n    \\n    printf(\\\"Validating clusters:\\\\n\\\");\\n    \\n    // Check each cluster\\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\\n        \\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\\n            }\\n        }\\n        \\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\\n            printf(\\\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\\\n\\\", \\n                   c, };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n//\n// Instead of recomputing the maximum distance to every member for every\n// candidate on every step, the per-candidate maximum is maintained\n// incrementally.  Candidates whose maximum distance already reached the\n// threshold can never become eligible again and are dropped.  The member\n// order and tie-breaking are identical to the straightforward version.\n//\n// The optional cardinality bound allows early termination once the cluster\n// can no longer become larger than the best candidate already found by this\n// thread (which is always positioned earlier than the current seed).\nvoid generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<char>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              const int cardinality_bound,\n                              std::vector<double>& max_dist,\n                              std::vector<int>& active,\n                              std::vector<int>& members) {\n    members.clear();\n    active.clear();\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    const Point& seed = points[seed_point];\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        if (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed_point) continue;\n\n        const double dist = distance(points[candidate], seed);\n        if (dist < threshold) {\n            max_dist[candidate] = dist;\n            active.push_back(candidate);\n        }\n    }\n\n    while (!active.empty()) {\n        if (static_cast<int>(members.size() + active.size()) <= cardinality_bound) {\n            break;\n        }\n\n        size_t best_index = 0;\n        double best_dist = max_dist[active[0]];\n        for (size_t i = 1; i < active.size(); ++i) {\n            const int candidate = active[i];\n            const double dist = max_dist[candidate];\n            if (dist < best_dist) {\n                best_dist = dist;\n                best_index = i;\n            }\n        }\n\n        const int chosen = active[best_index];\n        members.push_back(chosen);\n        active.erase(active.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(best_index));\n\n        const Point& chosen_point = points[chosen];\n        size_t write = 0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < active.size(); ++i) {\n            const int candidate = active[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], chosen_point);\n            if (dist > max_dist[candidate]) {\n                max_dist[candidate] = dist;\n            }\n            if (max_dist[candidate] < threshold) {\n                active[write++] = candidate;\n            }\n        }\n        active.resize(write);\n    }\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    int best_cardinality = -1;\n    int best_position = INT_MAX;\n    int best_seed = -1;\n    std::vector<int> best_members;\n    int finished = 0;\n\n    // The outer loop is inherently sequential (each iteration depends on the\n    // clusters formed so far), but all seed evaluations within an iteration\n    // are independent and dominate the runtime.  Threads are kept alive across\n    // outer iterations so their scratch buffers are allocated only once.\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        std::vector<double> max_dist(N);\n        std::vector<int> active;\n        std::vector<int> members;\n        std::vector<int> local_members;\n        active.reserve(N);\n        members.reserve(N);\n        local_members.reserve(N);\n\n        int local_cardinality = -1;\n        int local_position = INT_MAX;\n        int local_seed = -1;\n\n        while (true) {\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                finished = unclustered_indices.empty();\n            }\n            if (finished) break;\n\n            local_cardinality = -1;\n            local_position = INT_MAX;\n            local_seed = -1;\n\n            const int candidate_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n            // Try each unclustered point as a seed in parallel.  Dynamic\n            // scheduling balances the highly variable per-seed cost.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)\n            for (int i = 0; i < candidate_count; ++i) {\n                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n                if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n\n                generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,\n                                         local_cardinality, max_dist, active,\n                                         members);\n\n                const int cardinality = static_cast<int>(members.size());\n                if (cardinality > local_cardinality) {\n                    local_cardinality = cardinality;\n                    local_position = i;\n                    local_seed = seed;\n                    local_members = members;\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Merge thread-local winners.  Ties keep the earliest seed in\n            // unclustered_indices order, matching the sequential semantics.\n            #pragma omp critical\n            {\n                if (local_cardinality > best_cardinality ||\n                    (local_cardinality == best_cardinality &&\n                     local_position < best_position)) {\n                    best_cardinality = local_cardinality;\n                    best_position = local_position;\n                    best_seed = local_seed;\n                    best_members = local_members;\n                }\n            }\n            #pragma omp barrier\n\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                if (best_seed >= 0 && best_cardinality > 0) {\n                    Cluster cluster;\n                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n                    cluster.members.swap(best_members);\n                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n                    \n                    for (int member : clusters.back().members) {\n                        clustered[member] = 1;\n                    }\n                    \n                    unclustered_indices.erase(\n                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n                        unclustered_indices.end()\n                    );\n                } else {\n                    finished = 1;\n                }\n                best_cardinality = -1;\n                best_position = INT_MAX;\n                best_seed = -1;\n                best_members.clear();\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    \n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);\n    \n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n    \n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n    \n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n    \n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : \n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n    \n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\", \n           total_clustered, num_points, \n           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n    printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", avg_cluster_size);\n    printf(\"Maximum cluster size: %d\\n\", max_cluster_size);\n    \n    // Performance metrics\n    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\", \n           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n        std::vector<double> membershipData;\n        membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int m : membership) {\n            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));\n        }\n        print_results(membershipData, \"ClusterMembership\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n"},"output":"Wrote file successfully.","metadata":{"diagnostics":{},"filepath":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/qtclustering_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_omp_r4/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp","exists":true,"truncated":false},"title":"home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/qtclustering_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_omp_r4/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp","time":{"start":1792621204380,"end":1792621204462}},"id":"prt_1608cadd5001piInKBc38tDUO6","sessionID":"ses_e9f759b10ffe61KlatFzJ7FMRz","messageID":"msg_1608c2c790013mIybKPqTEOdnB"}}

Candidates whose maximum distance already reached the\n// threshold can never become eligible again and are dropped.  The member\n// order and tie-breaking are identical to the straightforward version.\n//\n// The optional cardinality bound allows early termination once the cluster\n// can no longer become larger than the best candidate already found by this\n// thread (which is always positioned earlier than the current seed).\nvoid generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<char>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              const int cardinality_bound,\n                              std::vector<double>& max_dist,\n                              std::vector<int>& active,\n                              std::vector<int>& members) {\n    members.clear();\n    active.clear();\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    const Point& seed = points[seed_point];\n    const double threshold_squared = threshold * threshold;\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        if (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed_point) continue;\n\n        const double dx = points[candidate].x - seed.x;\n        const double dy = points[candidate].y - seed.y;\n        const double dist_squared = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n        if (dist_squared < threshold_squared) {\n            max_dist[candidate] = dist_squared;\n            active.push_back(candidate);\n        }\n    }\n\n    while (!active.empty()) {\n        if (static_cast<int>(members.size() + active.size()) <= cardinality_bound) {\n            break;\n        }\n\n        size_t best_index = 0;\n        double best_dist = max_dist[active[0]];\n        for (size_t i = 1; i < active.size(); ++i) {\n            const int candidate = active[i];\n            const double dist = max_dist[candidate];\n            if (dist < best_dist) {\n                best_dist = dist;\n                best_index = i;\n            }\n        }\n\n        const int chosen = active[best_index];\n        members.push_back(chosen);\n        active.erase(active.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(best_index));\n\n        const Point& chosen_point = points[chosen];\n        size_t write = 0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < active.size(); ++i) {\n            const int candidate = active[i];\n            const double dx = points[candidate].x - chosen_point.x;\n            const double dy = points[candidate].y - chosen_point.y;\n            const double dist_squared = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n            if (dist_squared > max_dist[candidate]) {\n                max_dist[candidate] = dist_squared;\n            }\n            if (max_dist[candidate] < threshold_squared) {\n                active[write++] = candidate;\n            }\n        }\n        active.resize(write);\n    }\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);\n    for (int i = 0; Threads are kept alive across\n    // outer iterations so their scratch buffers are allocated only once.\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        std::vector<double> max_dist(N);\n        std::vector<int> active;\n        std::vector<int> members;\n        std::vector<int> local_members;\n        active.reserve(N);\n        members.reserve(N);\n        local_members.reserve(N);\n\n        int local_cardinality = -1;\n        int local_position = INT_MAX;\n        int local_seed = -1;\n\n        while (!finished) {\n            local_cardinality = -1;\n            local_position = INT_MAX;\n            local_seed = -1;\n\n            const int candidate_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n            // Try each unclustered point as a seed in parallel.  Dynamic\n            // scheduling balances the highly variable per-seed cost.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)\n            for (int i = 0; i < candidate_count; ++i) {\n                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n                if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n\n                // A cluster can never exceed the number of candidates still\n                // eligible, so stop as soon as it cannot beat the best local\n                // cluster.  Likewise it may stop once it cannot even reach the\n                // best cardinality observed anywhere by another thread.\n                int bound = shared_cardinality.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) - 1;\n                if (local_cardinality > bound) bound = local_cardinality;\n\n                generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,\n                                         bound, max_dist, active, members);\n\n                const int cardinality = static_cast<int>(members.size());\n                if (cardinality > local_cardinality) {\n                    local_cardinality = cardinality;\n                    local_position = i;\n                    local_seed = seed;\n                    local_members = members;\n\n                    int observed = shared_cardinality.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);\n                    while (cardinality > observed &&\n                           !shared_cardinality.compare_exchange_weak(\n                               observed, cardinality, std::memory_order_relaxed)) {\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Merge thread-local winners.  Ties keep the earliest seed in\n            // unclustered_indices order, matching the sequential semantics.\n            #pragma omp critical\n            {\n                if (local_cardinality > best_cardinality ||\n                    (local_cardinality == best_cardinality &&\n                     local_position < best_position)) {\n                    best_cardinality = local_cardinality;\n                    best_position = local_position;\n                    best_seed = local_seed;\n                    best_members = local_members;\n                }\n            }\n            #pragma omp barrier\n\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                if (best_seed >= 0 && best_cardinality > 0) {\n                    Cluster cluster;\n                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n                    cluster.members.swap(best_members);\n                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n                    \n                    for (int member : clusters.back().members) {\n                        clustered[member] = 1;\n                    }\n                    \n                    unclustered_indices.erase(\n                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0;
